# Sources index

Every place JGCT knowledge lives, how to query it, its tier, when it was last checked, and what was found. The catalogue below is maintained by hand; the ledger snapshot at the bottom is regenerated by `python3 tools/build_index.py`. Item-level status lives in the ledger (`python3 tools/ledger.py`).

**Rule since 2026-10-31:** a record is written from a source read in full or, when no open route to the full text exists, from its abstract alone (`evidence_basis: abstract`, marked on every citation; replaced when full text can be read). Ledger statuses: `found` (not yet examined), `processed` (record written from full text), `abstract_only` (record written from the abstract; full text still wanted), `needs_full_text` (relevant, but neither full text nor a usable abstract; the note lists the routes tried), `unreachable` (not even metadata), `skipped` (not relevant; note says why). On 2026-10-30 (second run) 175 earlier abstract-based records were withdrawn to `needs_full_text`. On 2026-10-30 (run 3) 359 untriaged items with JGCT in title or abstract (PubMed core query, OpenAlex) for which no full text was reachable were set to `needs_full_text`, so that status now lists the paywalled core literature.

## Catalogue

| Source | How to query | Tier of items | Last checked | What was found / status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PubMed (E-utilities) | `tools/fetch.py discover-pubmed "<query>"`; `tools/fetch.py abstract <pmid>` for verbatim abstracts | peer_reviewed (mostly) | 2026-10-30 | 2026-10-30: every ledger item with "juvenile granulosa" in the title was dispositioned (processed, unreachable or skipped). Queries run: JGCT title/abstract variants (462 hits); granulosa AND pediatric terms AND gonad (874); sex cord AND pediatric AND gonad; EXPeRT/PARTNER AND sex cord; author queries Kalfa N, Schneider DT. Core JGCT literature ~460 papers. |
| Europe PMC (REST) | `tools/fetch.py discover-epmc "<query>"`; `tools/fetch.py fulltext <pmid>` for OA full text; `tools/fetch.py cites <pmid> --dir citing|refs` | peer_reviewed, preprint, conference supplements | 2026-10-30 | `"juvenile granulosa"` full-text search: 877 hits (many only mention JGCT). Full text fetched for 40267023, 40272823, 26137586, 40350706, 21501861. Europe PMC fullTextXML returns HTTP 500 for some PMC IDs; fetcher falls back to NCBI PMC efetch. Citation chase run on 40267023, 40272823, 26137586, 32910017, 35031544. |
| NCBI PMC efetch and PMC website | efetch is used automatically by `fetch.py fulltext` and `fulltext.py get`; since 2026-10-30 (run 3) `fulltext.py` falls back to the PMC website article page (`https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC<id>/`, plain browser UA) | peer_reviewed, conference supplements | 2026-10-30 | efetch and Europe PMC fullTextXML give no body for PMC articles outside the OA subset (free-to-read, NIH author manuscripts, Wiley congress supplements). The PMC website serves those article pages. It answers bursts with reCAPTCHA; the tool backs off 30/90/180 s and never solves it, so queue at most one request every few seconds. Old scanned PMC articles (page images only) still have no text. Run 3 opened 20+ such articles and 4 congress supplements this way. |
| Europe PMC preprints (SRC:PPR) | `discover-epmc '"juvenile granulosa" AND SRC:PPR'`; abstract via Europe PMC REST `resultType=core` | preprint | 2026-10-30 | Processed: 3 preprint records (Authorea and Research Square case reports, a prepubertal testicular tumor ultrasound/pathology study); the Research Square version of pmid:33362514 skipped as duplicate. |
| ClinicalTrials.gov API v2 | `curl 'https://clinicaltrials.gov/api/v2/studies?query.cond=<term>'` (terms used: juvenile granulosa; granulosa cell tumor; sex cord stromal; ovarian stromal tumor; Sertoli-Leydig) | database | 2026-10-30 | No JGCT-specific interventional trial. Recorded NCT01970696 (IOTSTR), NCT05773651 (STEP 2.0), NCT01042522 (GOG adult SCST phase II), and on 2026-10-30 NCT03382158 (PPB/DICER1 Registry), NCT01572467 (DICER1 in pediatric SCST), NCT00006227 (paclitaxel second line, ovarian stromal), NCT02429700 (TC vs BEP, malignant SCST). Adult-GCT-only trials (GREKO, nirogacestat, etc.) not recorded. |
| Orphanet ORPHAcode API | `curl -H "apiKey: <any>" https://api.orphacode.org/EN/ClinicalEntity/...` | database | 2026-10-29 | No JGCT entity; only ORPHA:99915 (adult-type definition). Recorded. |
| OncoTree (MSK) | `https://oncotree.mskcc.org/api/tumorTypes/search/name/granulosa` | database | 2026-10-29 | Only GRCT (Granulosa Cell Tumor), no juvenile code. Not recorded (no clinical content). |
| cBioPortal | `tools/cbio_grct_scan.py` then `tools/cbio_grct_detail.py <study>` (public API https://www.cbioportal.org/api) | database | 2026-10-30 | 551 public studies scanned: one sample explicitly labelled JGCT; GRCT samples mostly FOXL2 C134W (adult type); one 7-year-old FOXL2-wild-type AKT1-insertion GRCT. Recorded as url-6657783a29d4. AACR GENIE instance (genie.cbioportal.org) returns HTTP 401. |
| NCI PDQ (cancer.gov) | curl the HTML of `/types/ovarian/hp/child-ovarian-treatment-pdq`, strip tags | organization | 2026-10-29 | Childhood Ovarian Cancer Treatment PDQ (updated 2025-03-03); JGCT section recorded. |
| Trial registries outside the US (EU CTR, CTIS, ISRCTN, JRCT, ChiCTR) | EU CTR search page `https://www.clinicaltrialsregister.eu/ctr-search/search?query=granulosa`; ISRCTN `https://www.isrctn.com/api/query/format/default?q=granulosa`; CTIS public search API; JRCT/ChiCTR web search | database | 2026-10-30 | Probed by curl. EU CTR: adult GCT trials only (GREKO orteronel, nirogacestat). ISRCTN: 5 hits, none JGCT (one basket trial lists a granulosa sub-group). CTIS: adult-type GCT only. JRCT/ChiCTR returned only search shells (JavaScript); not usable without a browser. No JGCT trial found; nothing recorded. |
| Conference abstracts (ASCO, ESPE, ECP, ESPR, APPES, SIOP, ASPHO, SGO, USCAP) | OpenAlex (`tools/openalex_discover.py`, types conference-abstract/paper); `tools/epmc_supplement_grep.py <PMCID>` for supplements in PMC (XML if OA, else PMC website text with windows around matches); Europe PMC query `"juvenile granulosa" AND (TITLE:abstracts OR TITLE:congress OR TITLE:meeting OR TITLE:proceedings)` lists supplements; ESPE at abstracts.eurospe.org | conference_abstract | 2026-10-30 | Run 3: SIOP 2014 (PMC7168141: TGM95 O-056, testicular EP-686), SIOP 2017 (PMC7167988: P-284), SIOP 2018 (PMC7167781: MAKEI/STEP O-104) and ASPHO 2016 (PMC7167840: OTST poster 701) read and recorded. Not yet searched: ECP 1999 (PMC7102193), ECP 2003 (PMC7087991), Dublin Pathology 2015 (PMC7168113), Turkish 2018 meeting (PMC6078391), Ochsner 2013 (PMC3776526); SIOP 2019 onward supplements are not in PMC. SGO, USCAP, ASPHO other years not searched systematically. |
| GeneReviews (DICER1 tumor predisposition) | NCBI Bookshelf NBK196157 (HTML via curl) | database | 2026-10-30 | Recorded url-1b49311e69d4 (full text): JGCT rarely associated with DICER1; testing especially if anaplasia or high mitotic index; surveillance pelvic ultrasound every 6 months in carriers. |
| COSMIC / AACR GENIE | GENIE via genie.cbioportal.org (login); COSMIC requires login | database | 2026-10-30 | GENIE API returned 401; not queried. COSMIC not attempted. |
| OpenAlex | `tools/openalex_discover.py fetch '' out.jsonl 'title_and_abstract.search:juvenile granulosa'` then `compare out.jsonl .` | mixed | 2026-10-30 | 731 works with JGCT in title/abstract; 295 not matched to the ledger, of which 264 loaded as `found` (origin `openalex 2026-10-30`, priority 40; book chapters and reference entries priority 10). Some are DOI-keyed duplicates of PubMed items whose DOI→PMID mapping failed; dedupe before processing. |
| Full-text routes | `python3 -I tools/fulltext.py get <id>`: PMC XML (Europe PMC, NCBI efetch) → PMC website page → Unpaywall OA locations → DOI landing page; `--url` for a PDF/page found elsewhere (OpenAlex `locations`, Semantic Scholar `openAccessPdf`, repository records) | — | 2026-10-30 | Works: PMC OA XML, PMC website for non-OA PMC, many small OA journals (PDF), institutional repositories, KoreaScience PDFs, OJS galleys (`article/download/<id>/<galley>`). Blocked to scripts: Elsevier/ScienceDirect and journal sites on Elsevier hosting (captcha), Wiley pdfdirect, OUP, AACR, ASCO, LWW, BMJ, Allen Press (403), Authorea (403), Cambridge Core chapters (landing page only), JCDR article pages (site shell; JCDR PDFs sometimes reachable through PMC). Persian PDFs with legacy font encoding extract as garbled text (needs OCR). Run 3 also tried OpenAlex locations and Semantic Scholar for all needs_full_text DOIs: 43 had an alternative URL, most of them blocked publisher PDFs. |
| Rare disease organizations (NORD, GARD) | web | organization | never | Backlog; low priority. |
| Unpaywall | `https://api.unpaywall.org/v2/<doi>?email=...` to find legal OA copies of paywalled papers | — | 2026-10-30 | Found the CC-BY 2026 European recommendations (pmid-42504512) in the Padua repository. EXPeRT 2021 and ESGO-SIOPE 2020 closed; the EXPeRT Authorea preprint returns 403 to scripts. |
| Semantic Scholar (bulk search API) | `https://api.semanticscholar.org/graph/v1/paper/search/bulk?query="juvenile granulosa"&fields=title,year,externalIds,openAccessPdf,venue` (free, paginate with `token`) | mixed; regional journals, non-English | 2026-10-31 | 664 items; 173 not in PubMed or Europe PMC (Spanish, Portuguese, Chinese, Japanese, Persian, Russian, Indonesian, Korean items, many with `openAccessPdf`). Compared with the ledger by DOI and title: 29 were new and were loaded as `found` (origin `maintainer review 2026-10-31 (...)`, priority 30); the rest were already present from the OpenAlex load. Full list in `state/reviews/2026-10-31-leads-outside-pubmed-epmc.tsv`. Screen titles first; the bulk search also returns unrelated papers. |
| Crossref | `https://api.crossref.org/works?query.bibliographic="juvenile granulosa"&rows=1000` | mixed | 2026-10-31 | Mostly duplicates of PubMed items. Two 1990s meeting abstracts (liver-directed therapy, autologous transplant) not found elsewhere; in the leads file. |
| DOAJ | `https://doaj.org/api/search/articles/"juvenile granulosa"` | peer_reviewed (small OA journals) | 2026-10-31 | 78 items, about 20 not in the ledger, all with usable OA full text. In the leads file. |
| J-STAGE and CiNii (Japan) | `https://api.jstage.jst.go.jp/searchapi/do?service=3&text=juvenile granulosa`; `https://cir.nii.ac.jp/opensearch/all?q=juvenile granulosa&format=json` | peer_reviewed | 2026-10-31 | Japanese case reports with English abstracts, some OA PDFs (one recorded: doi-10.5180_jsgoe.32.285). Not yet processed systematically. |
| SciELO (Latin America) | `https://search.scielo.org/?q="juvenile granulosa"&output=ris` | peer_reviewed | 2026-10-31 | Spanish and Portuguese case reports (Chile, Brazil, Venezuela); OA PDFs. In the leads file. |
| CNKI / Wanfang (China) | no public API; Chinese titles surface through OpenAlex and Semantic Scholar | peer_reviewed | 2026-10-31 | Chinese series exist only as English abstracts here (e.g. pmid-42706059); full Chinese text would need a reader. |
| Educational and reference sites (PathologyOutlines, Radiopaedia, MyPathologyReport, Wikipedia) | PathologyOutlines `ovarytumorgctjuv`, `testisgranulosajuvenile`; Radiopaedia; MyPathologyReport; Wikipedia | organization / secondary | 2026-10-31 | Secondary sources, not recorded; useful as cross-checks and as the pages families land on. PathologyOutlines (429) and Radiopaedia (406) block scripts: read by hand. |
| Registries' own sites | OTST Registry (otstregistry.org, Children's Minnesota); STEP (raretumors-children.eu); International PPB/DICER1 Registry (ppbregistry.org); TESST group | organization | 2026-10-31 | Enrolment and contact information; the trial-registry entries for the same registries are recorded (url-599767ff9c45, url-00efe8fac0ba, url-f623903cbc28). Re-check yearly for new reports. |
| Patient communities | GCT Survivor Sisters (closed Facebook group, admits parents of JGCT patients); Inspire ovarian cancer community; Ovarian Cancer Research Alliance | organization | 2026-10-31 | No JGCT-specific charity exists. Not recorded (no citable content); named in the families topic on where to turn only as what exists. |
| ESPE abstracts | abstracts.eurospe.org (search "granulosa") | conference_abstract | 2026-10-31 | Paediatric endocrinology case abstracts (precocious puberty presentations). Not yet read systematically. |
| Theses and dissertations | OATD (`https://oatd.org/oatd/search?q=juvenile granulosa`), BASE, ProQuest (metadata only) | thesis | never | Not yet searched. |
| WHO Classification of Tumours (Blue Books, 5th ed., Female Genital Tumours 2020; Urinary and Male Genital Tumours 2022) | not public (subscription) | reference | 2026-10-31 | The defining text; reached only through reviews that quote it (pmid-38136408, pmid-40528632). |
| Non-English literature | PubMed returns Chinese, German, French items with English abstracts | peer_reviewed | 2026-10-29 | Chinese (42706059, 39863551) and German (33201299, 18008216) items recorded from English abstracts. |

## Notes for future runs

- WebFetch returns a paraphrase; use the APIs above for anything cited.
- Ledger IDs: `pmid:<n>`, `doi:<lowercase doi>`, `epmc:<SRC>:<id>`, `nct:<NCT id>`, `orpha:<code>`, `url:<short>`.
- Title-keyword priorities in `fetch.py` are crude; many priority-75 "guideline" hits are unrelated (pancreatic, testicular germ cell). Screen titles and mark `skipped` with a note.
